## 新投稿 (70)

<h3 id="1">[1] Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18801](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
- **Authors**: Shiqiang Wang, Herbert Woisetschläger, Hans Arno Jacobsen, Mingyue Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Data Synthesis, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出开发数据探针（data probes）的方法论，通过从随机过程生成合成序列来系统研究数据特征如何影响LLM在训练、调优和推理等阶段的行为。作者主张利用典型集等理论概念，超越经验启发式方法，为理解数据在LLM中的作用提供基础性见解。

<h3 id="2">[2] Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18818](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
- **Authors**: Yao Fehlis, Benjamin Bengfort, Zhangzhang Si, Vahid Eyorokon, Prema Roman, Patrick Deziel, Devon Slonaker, Steve Veldman, Ben Johnson, Joyce Rigelo, Michael Wharton, Steve Kramer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, LLM Deployment, OCR
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种微服务架构，用于生产环境下的文档理解流水线，整合分类、OCR和LLM结构化字段提取。研究发现OCR而非语言模型解析主导端到端延迟，系统饱和由GPU推理容量而非工作线程数决定。

<h3 id="3">[3] Evaluating the Utility of Personal Health Records in Personalized Health AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18937)
- **Authors**: Rory Sayres, Kejia Chen, Ayush Jain, Matthew Thompson, Jonathan Richina, Xiang Yin, Jimmy Hu, Fan Zhang, Bob Lou, Mike Sanchez, Ines Mezerreg, Meredith Schreier, Hamsa Subramaniam, I-Ching Lee, Yugang Jia, Daniel Mcduff, Yossi Matias, Avinatan Hassidim, Dale Webster, Yun Liu, Jackie Barr, Quang Duong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, Personalization
- **Summary**: 本研究评估LLM在结合个人健康记录（PHR）上下文时回答健康查询的能力，涉及2,257个用户查询。结果显示PHR数据显著提升回答的有用性，同时识别出LLM在理解复杂PHR方面的差距，如时间错位和罕见但有意义的幻觉。

<h3 id="4">[4] Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19008](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
- **Authors**: Anis Radianis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Optimization, Training Stability
- **Summary**: 本文提出LBW-Guard，一个在AdamW优化器之上运行的训练控制治理层，通过有界控制来应对LLM训练中的不稳定性。实验表明该方法在压力条件下将困惑度降低18.7%，并在学习率压力测试中保持稳定。

<h3 id="5">[5] AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
- **Authors**: Olena Bogdanov, Yeunji Jung, Chandra Dhir, Pareekshitreddy Gaddam, Saurabh Jain, Lakshmi Tumati, Vijay Parthasarathy, Anup Shirgaonkar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文提出一种多智能体NL2SQL方法，在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率。该方法利用语义增强的模式表示和用户提供的业务规则，通过优化的编排器进行规划、反思和自纠正来生成准确的SQL查询。

<h3 id="6">[6] KAN-MLP-Mixer: A comprehensive investigation of the usage of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) for improving IMU-based Human Activity Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19031)
- **Authors**: Mengxi Liu, Sizhen Bian, Vitor Fortes, Francisco Calatrava Nicolas, Daniel Geißler, Maximilian Kiefer-Emmanouilidis, Bo Zhou, Paul Lukowicz
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Human Activity Recognition, Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文系统研究Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）在IMU人体活动识别中的应用，提出混合KAN-MLP架构。该架构结合KAN的精确性与MLP的噪声鲁棒性，在八个HAR数据集上相比纯MLP模型平均提升5.33%的宏F1分数。

<h3 id="7">[7] Trustworthy Agent Network: Trust in Agent Networks Must Be Baked In, Not Bolted On</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19035](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19035)
- **Authors**: Yixiang Yao, Yuhang Yao, Xinyi Fan, Jiechao Gao, Jie Wang, Minjia Zhang, Srivatsan Ravi, Carlee Joe-Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, AI Safety
- **Venue**: SIGKDD 2026
- **Summary**: 本文论证Agent-to-Agent网络的信任性必须从一开始就架构设计，而非事后修补。作者提出了一个包含四个设计支柱的概念框架，用于解决协作智能体生态系统中的对抗性组合、语义错位和级联故障等系统性漏洞。

<h3 id="8">[8] Interference-Aware Multi-Task Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19042)
- **Authors**: Ying-Hua Huang, Rui Fang, Hsi-Wen Chen, Ming-Syan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Multi-Task Learning, Privacy
- **Summary**: 本文研究多任务遗忘学习，提出干扰感知框架，结合任务感知梯度投影和实例级梯度正交化。该方法在两个多任务计算机视觉基准上实现有效遗忘，同时将非目标任务的干扰分别降低30.3%和52.9%。

<h3 id="9">[9] Embedding by Elicitation: Dynamic Representations for Bayesian Optimization of System Prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19093](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19093)
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Jerry Lin, Benjamin Letham, Samuel Dooley, Maximilian Balandat, Eytan Bakshy
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, Bayesian Optimization, LLM Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出ReElicit框架，用于在仅有聚合反馈的情况下优化系统提示词。该方法利用LLM动态构建语义特征空间，结合高斯过程代理进行贝叶斯优化，在十个系统提示词优化任务中表现优异。

<h3 id="10">[10] DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19099](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Gao, Megan Wang, Yi Ling Yu, Zijian Carl Ma, Ao Qu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文引入DecisionBench基准，用于评估长时程智能体工作流中的涌现委托行为。研究发现仅关注质量的评估会遗漏编排信号，且完美委托比当前性能高出15-31个百分点，揭示了未来编排方法的巨大提升空间。

<h3 id="11">[11] POLAR-Bench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Privacy-Utility Trade-offs in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19127](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19127)
- **Authors**: Qiaoyuan Zheng, Yiqu Yang, Qi Gao, Imanol Schlag
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Privacy, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文提出POLAR-Bench基准，用于评估LLM智能体在与对抗性第三方系统交互时的隐私-效用权衡。结果显示前沿模型能保护99%以上的受保护属性，而1-30B参数的开源模型表现明显较差，最弱的泄露超过一半。

<h3 id="12">[12] Learning to Hand Off: Provably Convergent Workflow Learning under Interface Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19140](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19140)
- **Authors**: Jiayu Li, Enpei Zhang, Dawei Zhou, Elynn Chen, Yujun Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文将跨组织边界的多智能体LLM流水线形式化为接口约束半马尔可夫决策过程，提出IC-Q异步分散式Q学习算法。该算法在分散部分可观测条件下首次提供神经Q学习的有限样本保证。

<h3 id="13">[13] Progressive Autonomy as Preference Learning: A Formalization of Trust Calibration for Agentic Tool Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19151)
- **Authors**: Changkun Ou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Trust Calibration, Preference Learning, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文将智能体工具使用的信任校准形式化为偏好学习问题，使用高斯过程后验建模人类风险容忍度。该方法将问题框架为偏好贝叶斯优化，用于将动作空间分类为允许/阻止/询问区域。

<h3 id="14">[14] How Far Are We From True Auto-Research?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19156)
- **Authors**: Zhengxin Zhang, Ning Wang, Sainyam Galhotra, Claire Cardie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 本文引入ResearchArena评估智能体生成的研究论文质量，发现虽然智能体能产出在稿件评审中看似有竞争力的论文，但均未达到顶会接收标准。实验严谨性是主要瓶颈，不同智能体的失败模式差异显著。

<h3 id="15">[15] Discoverable Agent Knowledge -- A Formal Framework for Agentic KG Affordances (Extended Version)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19186](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19186)
- **Authors**: Terry R. Payne, Valentina Tamma, Enrico Daga
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, LLM Agent, Knowledge Representation
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个四维形式框架，用于知识图谱的智能体可供性描述，扩展VoID和DCAT元数据标准。该框架支持智能体规划时的知识图谱选择、组合和故障诊断，并识别了实现大规模可供性匹配的研究议程。

<h3 id="16">[16] Hallucination as Exploit: Evidence-Carrying Multimodal Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19192](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19192)
- **Authors**: Guijia Zhang, Hao Zheng, Harry Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Hallucination, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 本文将幻觉到动作的转换形式化为安全失效模式，提出证据携带多模态智能体（ECA）架构。ECA要求外部证据授权工具调用，在1900次攻击测试中将门控绕过率从15%降至1.3%，在端到端流水线中实现0%不安全动作率。

<h3 id="17">[17] Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
- **Authors**: Payam Piray
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Exploration Strategy, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 本文研究了环境不确定性中波动性和随机性对探索行为的相反影响，发现波动性促进探索而随机性抑制探索。作者提出了CAUSE框架，通过控制即推理方法获得闭式探索奖励，在异构噪声环境中优于标准探索策略。

<h3 id="18">[18] SimGym: A Framework for A/B Test Simulation in E-Commerce with Traffic-Grounded VLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19219)
- **Authors**: Han Li, Vibhor Malik, Zahra Zanjani Foumani, Alberto Castelo, Shuang Xie, Ailin Fan, Keat Yang Koay, Yuanzheng Zhu, Meysam Feghhi, Ronie Uliana, Zhaoyu Zhang, Angelo Ocana Martins, Mingyu Zhao, Francis Pelland, Jonathan Faerman, Nikolas LeBlanc, Aaron Glazer, Andrew McNamara, Zhong Wu, Lingyun Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, User Simulation, A/B Testing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了SimGym框架，利用视觉语言模型(VLM)代理在实时浏览器中模拟电商A/B测试。该框架包含基于生产点击流数据的流量导向角色生成管道、实时浏览器代理架构和评估协议，实现了77%的方向一致性，将实验周期从数周缩短至一小时以内。

<h3 id="19">[19] Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19229](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
- **Authors**: Yan Wang, Ziyi Guo, Christopher McCarty
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Data Imputation, Survey Research
- **Summary**: 本文评估了LLM在调查研究全流程中的应用，包括问卷设计、样本选择、试点测试、缺失数据插补和后收集分析。提出的锚定边际理论信息LLM(A-TLM)在灾难相关的块状MNAR条件下优于传统插补方法，同时实现了接近零的符号偏差。

<h3 id="20">[20] Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文评估了LLM在灾害准备调查全流程中的应用，提出了基于保护动机理论的A-TLM缺失数据填补方法。该方法在块状MNAR条件下优于传统填补基线，同时提出了子群体分层偏差审计标准。

<h3 id="21">[21] AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees</h3>

<h3 id="28">[28] What and When to Distill: Selective Hindsight Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents</h3>

<h3 id="65">[65] Probing Embodied LLMs: When Higher Observation Fidelity Hurts Problem Solving</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO

<h3 id="67">[67] Using Aristotle API for AI-Assisted Theorem Proving in Lean 4: A Formalisation Case Study of the Grasshopper Problem</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO

<h3 id="69">[69] HaorFloodAlert: Deseasonalized ML Ensemble for 72-Hour Flood Prediction in Bangladesh Haor Wetlands</h3>

<h3 id="80">[80] DualView: Adaptive Local-Global Fusion for Multi-Hop Document Reranking</h3>

<h3 id="81">[81] ClusterRAG: Cluster-Based Collaborative Filtering for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18769) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gibson Nkhata, Uttamasha Anjally Oyshi, Quan Mai, Susan Gauch
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, Personalization, Collaborative Filtering
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出ClusterRAG，一种基于聚类的协同过滤方法用于个性化RAG，通过将用户组织成语义一致的聚类并在聚类和文档两个层次进行检索。实验表明，联合利用目标用户和相似用户的画像信息在多样化任务中取得最佳性能。

<h3 id="82">[82] Agentic GraphRAG: Navigating Unstructured Financial Data with Collaborative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arthur Capozzi, Dirk Helbing
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个协作式代理GraphRAG框架用于商业注册数据分析，通过三阶段流水线构建Neo4j知识图谱，并集成分析模块化代理实现零样本意图路由和状态感知响应合成。在瑞士商业公报数据集上的实验表明该系统显著优于标准向量RAG基线。

<h3 id="83">[83] Improving Retrieval-Augmented Generation without Taxonomy-based Error Categorization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18772](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18772) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gongbo Zhang, Yifan Peng, Chunhua Weng
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出RePAIR，一种响应-动作学习范式，无需依赖细粒度错误分类即可直接将缺陷RAG输出映射到错误缓解动作计划。多个基准测试表明该方法持续改进代理式RAG性能。

<h3 id="84">[84] Decentralized autonomous organization and blockchain-based incentivization framework for community-based facilities management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18773) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reachsak Ly, Alireza Shojaei, Xinghua Gao, Philip Agee, Abiola Akanmu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Blockchain, Smart Home, Governance
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个基于区块链和去中心化自治组织(DAO)的社区设施管理框架，包含去中心化治理平台和维护管理激励平台。评估表明该框架能有效激励利益相关者参与设施维护。

<h3 id="85">[85] M3DocDep: Multi-modal, Multi-page, Multi-document Dependency Chunking with Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18774) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joongmin Shin, Jeongbae Park, Jaehyung Seo, Heuiseok Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Document Understanding, Vision-Language Model

- **Summary**: 本文提出M3DocDep，一种基于LVLM的流水线，通过恢复块级依赖关系并构建树引导的文档块来改进多页文档的RAG检索。实验表明该方法在检索和问答任务上显著提升性能。

<h3 id="86">[86] Query-Aware Flow Diffusion for Graph-Based RAG with Retrieval Guarantees</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18775](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18775) (cross-listed)

- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出QAFD-RAG，一种无训练框架，通过查询感知的图遍历动态调整边权重，并提供统计保证的相关子图检索。在问答和Text-to-SQL任务上持续优于现有图RAG方法。

<h3 id="87">[87] Mask-to-Correct$^+$: Leveraging Retriever Diversity for Masking-guided Faithful Fact Correction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18776](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18776) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Payel Santra, Lavisha Sharma, Madhusudan Ghosh, Partha Basuchowdhuri
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Fact Checking, Misinformation
- **Summary**: 本文提出M2C和M2C+，一种无训练的RAG框架用于事实纠正，利用多样性感知掩码识别错误片段并结合多排序器集成提高鲁棒性。实验显示SARI分数提升达14%。

<h3 id="88">[88] A Reproducibility Analysis of PO4ISR: Diagnosing and Mitigating Semantic Drift in LLM-Based Session Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18780) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Tiwari, Konduri Naga Lakshmi Rekha, Rajesh Kumar Mundotiya
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Research Reproducibility, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文对PO4ISR进行可复现性研究，发现标准推理提示在长会话中存在语义漂移问题。作者提出PO4ISR++，通过反思性提示和一致性排名检测增强鲁棒性，在Games和Bundle数据集上取得显著提升。

<h3 id="89">[89] Can LLMs Emulate Human Belief Dynamics?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18781](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18781) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adiba Mahbub Proma, Neeley Pate, James N. Druckman, Gourab Ghoshal, Hangfeng He, Ehsan Hoque
- **Subjects**: cs.SI; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Social Simulation, LLM Evaluation, Social Network Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文评估12个LLM在模拟社交网络中人类信念动态的能力，发现LLM无法捕捉初始信念分布且比人类更顺从。研究结果警告不应将LLM作为社交模拟中的人类代理。

<h3 id="90">[90] The Insurability Frontier of AI Risk: Mapping Threats to Affirmative Coverage, Silent Exposures, and Exclusions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18784](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18784) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alex Leung, Rex Zhang, Ervin Ling, Kentaroh Toyoda, SiewMei Loh
- **Subjects**: q-fin.RM; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.CY; econ.GN
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Risk Analysis, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 本文将55类AI威胁映射到26种保险产品，识别出四层可保性边界：明确承保风险、沉默AI暴露、主动排除风险和传统保险之外的风险。分析揭示了AI保险分化的模式和基础模型集中度作为新型可保性前沿。

<h3 id="91">[91] Features have life history. And we should care</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18789) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Philipp Stecher, Sandro Radovanović, Vlasta Sikimić, Reinhard Kahle
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Representation Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文发现Pythia模型中存在约50个具有稳定生命周期的稀疏特征构成的载体支架，它们在训练早期组装完成并承载模型表示结构。分析表明该支架具有承重性，且功能先于几何结构确定。

<h3 id="92">[92] Dimensional Balance Improves Large Scale Spatiotemporal Prediction Performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18793](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18793) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jing Chen, Shixiang Pan, Yujie Fan, Haocheng Ye, Haitao Xu, Wenqiang Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spatiotemporal Forecasting, Time Series Forecasting, Urban Computing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ST-Balance/ST-Balance)
- **Summary**: 本文提出一个可扩展的自适应框架，通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度并扩展时间范围来协调时空特征表示。在城市交通、气象和流行病数据集上的实验表明该方法显著提升预测精度。

<h3 id="93">[93] Robust Basis Spline Decoupling for the Compression of Transformer Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18794) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joppe De Jonghe, Van Tien Pham, Mariya Ishteva
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Transformer Architecture, Neural Compression
- **Summary**: 本文引入基于B样条的解耦框架用于神经网络压缩，利用B样条的局部支持和灵活平滑控制提供数值稳定且富有表达力的表示。在Vision和Swin Transformer上的实验表明该方法在保持精度的同时实现显著参数压缩。

<h3 id="94">[94] HELLoRA: Hot Experts Layer-Level Low-Rank Adaptation for Mixture-of-Experts Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18795](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18795) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jia Wei, Zhonghao Zhang, Ping Chen, Qianyang li, Yancheng Pan, Shaoxun Wang, Ziyi Qiu, Longxiang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Training

<h3 id="95">[95] Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18797](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18797) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rao Fu, Zixuan Yang, Jiankun Zhang, Jing Ma, Hechang Chen, Yu Li, Yi Chang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, LLM Training, LLM Inference

<h3 id="96">[96] ReCrit: Transition-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Scientific Critic Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18799](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18799) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wanghan Xu, Yuhao Zhou, Hengyuan Zhao, Shuo Li, Dianzhi Yu, Zhenfei Yin, Yaowen Hu, Fengli Xu, Wanli Ouyang, Wenlong Zhang, Lei Bai

- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/black-yt/ReCrit)

<h3 id="97">[97] Theory-optimal Quantization Based on Flatness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18800](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18800) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiusheng Huang, Zhe Li, Xuanwu Yin, Lu Wang, Yequan Wang, Dong Li, Emad Barsoum, Kang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, LLM Inference, Model Compression

<h3 id="98">[98] A Nonlinear Complexity Index for Wearable PPG Cardiovascular Stability: Multiscale Validation, Systematic Evaluation Correction, and Bayesian Parameter Optimization</h3>

- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于心脏稳定性理论的稳定性约束心血管稳定性指数(SCSI)，在四个PPG数据集上进行了多尺度验证，识别并纠正了三种评估伪影，通过贝叶斯优化实现了跨人群泛化的心血管稳定性评估。

<h3 id="99">[99] PROWL: Prioritized Regret-Driven Optimization for World Model Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18803](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18803) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="100">[100] Adaptive Multi-Scale Goodness Aggregation for Forward-Forward Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18804](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18804) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Salar Beigzad, Vansh Verma
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了自适应多尺度优度聚合(AMSGA)方法，通过引入多尺度优度聚合、自适应课程引导的困难负样本挖掘和层级自适应阈值，扩展了Forward-Forward算法，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上分别实现了1.45%和1.50%的性能提升。

<h3 id="101">[101] RecoAtlas: From Semantic Plausibility to Set-Level Utility in LLM Recommendation Agents</h3>

<h3 id="102">[102] Block-Based Double Decoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18807](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18807) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Asher Labovich, Benjamin Bradley, Vanessa Alexander, Chaitanya Harsha
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="103">[103] Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18808](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18808) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joao Paulo Cavalcante Presa, Savio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent Coordination
- **Summary**: 本文提出了HPML(霍奇投影多智能体学习)方法，将多智能体系统的联合更新场投影到最近的度量梯度势流上，通过图实现和神经实现恢复投影方向，证明了投影动力学具有Lyapunov势函数并提供均衡间隙界限。

<h3 id="104">[104] Metric-Gradient Projection for Stable Multi-Agent Policy Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18809) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zuyuan Zhang, Sizhe Tang, Mahdi Imani, Tian Lan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference, LLM Optimization

<h3 id="105">[105] D-PACE: Dynamic Position-Aware Cross-Entropy for Parallel Speculative Drafting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18810](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18810) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyu Wu, Yu Yao, Zhenting Qi, Han Zheng, Zhuohan Wang, Haoran Ma, Lawrence Liao, Himabindu Lakkaraju, Ju Li, Yilun Du
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Diffusion Model, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个基于扩散的框架，通过对比专家乘积公式集成异构记忆模型，包括捕获局部动态的短期记忆专家、通过轻量级测试时微调存储情节历史的长期记忆专家，以及强制几何空间一致性的空间长期记忆专家。

<h3 id="106">[106] Composition of Memory Experts for Diffusion World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18813](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18813) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sebastian Stapf, Pablo Acuaviva Huertos, Aram Davtyan, Paolo Favaro
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LLM Training, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Hybrid-LoRA混合后训练框架，在固定参数预算下选择性地对不适合低秩适应的模块应用全微调，同时用LoRA适应其余组件，引入Hybrid-LoRA分数来排序候选模块对其低秩适应敏感度，在10%全微调模块预算下接近全微调性能。

<h3 id="107">[107] Symmetry in the Wild: The Role of Equivariance in Neural Fluid Surrogates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18816](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18816) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Patryk Rygiel, Julian Suk, Kak Khee Yeung, Christoph Brune, Jelmer M. Wolterink
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 本文将注意力交互矩阵分解为路由(斜对称)和过滤(对称)两个组件，发现路由在低秩下运行，引入S-D注意力作为诊断参数化来解耦路由和过滤，揭示了注意力可以简化的位置：前七层的线性化代价不到5%的困惑度。

<h3 id="108">[108] Emergence of Frontier Superposition: Möbius attractor and Cascade Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18820](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18820) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongyu Gu, Jingwen Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文研究了Transformer如何通过叠加推理实现图可达性，识别出Möbius吸引子架构和级联监督作为关键机制。研究表明端到端监督无法满足梯度持久性条件，而级联监督能够使梯度下降找到最优叠加状态。

<h3 id="109">[109] Hybrid-LoRA: Bridging Full Fine-Tuning and Low-Rank Adaptation for Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18822) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengqian Zhang, Wei Zhu, Kyumin Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Hybrid-LoRA，一种混合后训练框架，选择性地对不适合低秩适应的模块应用全量微调，其余组件使用LoRA。引入了Hybrid-LoRA分数来排序候选模块，在10%全量微调模块预算下实现了接近全量微调的性能。

<h3 id="110">[110] Fine-Grained Benchmark Generation for Comprehensive Evaluation of Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18824](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18824) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Saidul Islam, Negin Baghbanzadeh, Farnaz Kohankhaki, Afshin Cheraghi, Ali Kore, Shayaan Mehdi, Elham Dolatabadi, Arash Afkanpour
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Evaluation, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了一个自动化基准生成框架，使用教科书等参考材料生成具有广泛覆盖、丰富元数据和抗污染能力的评估问题。生成的机器学习、企业金融和个人金融基准在专家审查中显示出比MMLU和GSM8K更低的真值错误率。

<h3 id="111">[111] The Routing and Filtering Structure of Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18826](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18826) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shafayeth Jamil, Rehan Kapadia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 本文将注意力交互矩阵分解为路由(斜对称)和过滤(对称)两个组件，发现路由以低秩方式运行。提出的S-D注意力参数化实现了稳定的无层归一化训练，揭示了谱级联模式，预测了注意力可以线性化的位置。

<h3 id="112">[112] Precision Tracked Transformer via Kalman Filtering, Kriging and Process Noise</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18832](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18832) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Uncertainty Estimation, Bayesian Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出了贝叶斯滤波变换器(BFT)，将注意力变为精度加权克里金，残差连接变为带自适应增益的卡尔曼更新，FFN变为通过雅可比加过程噪声规则传播精度的动力学模型，在序列推荐和监督微调中显著提升了冷启动用户和稀有物品的性能。

<h3 id="113">[113] Automated Big Data Quality Assessment using Knowledge Graph Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18833) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hadi Fadlallah, Rima Kilany, Mitri Haber, Ali Jaber
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Data Quality Assessment, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于知识图谱嵌入的自动化数据质量评估方法，通过预测输入数据集上下文表示与质量规则之间的缺失边来生成上下文特定的数据质量评估计划。该方法整合了多样化的表示形式，克服了传统方法仅依赖严格匹配而忽略上下文特征的局限性。

<h3 id="114">[114] VCR: Learning Valid Contextual Representation for Incomplete Wearable Signals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18837) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Weng, Wenhan Luo, Qijia Shao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Wearable Computing, Multimodal Learning, Healthcare Monitoring
- **Summary**: 本文提出了VCR框架，一种针对可穿戴设备信号模态缺失问题的自监督学习方法。该方法通过正交分词器将每种模态分离为共享语义和模态特定残差，并使用缺失感知的混合专家骨干网络来适应不同的模态可用性模式。

<h3 id="115">[115] Lying Is Just a Phase: The Hidden Alignment Transition in Language Model Scaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18838) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adil Amin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Scaling Laws, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/adilamin89/cape-scaling)
- **Summary**: 本文发现语言模型在扩展过程中存在一个隐藏的相变现象：在临界规模以下，推理能力与真实性负相关；超过临界规模后，两者开始协同。作者分析了架构、数据筛选和训练方案对该临界点的影响，并提供了诊断工具。

<h3 id="116">[116] An Integrated Forecasting Prototype for Emergency Department Boarding Time to Support Proactive Operational Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18839](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18839) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Orhun Vural, Abdulaziz Ahmed, Ferhat Zengul, James Booth, Bunyamin Ozaydin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Healthcare Monitoring, Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 本文开发了一个多时间视野的时间序列预测框架，用于预测急诊科住院等待时间，支持主动运营决策。该框架整合了天气、节假日等外部上下文数据源，并开发了MLOps Web应用原型以支持实际部署。

<h3 id="117">[117] The Growing Pains of Frontier Models: When Leaderboards Stop Separating and What to Measure Next</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18840](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18840) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adil Amin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Scaling Laws
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/adilamin89/cape-scaling)
- **Summary**: 本文分析了前沿模型能力之间的交互关系，发现能力在整体上呈现协同关系，但各实验室和不同时期存在差异。作者提供了三级诊断框架、实验室测量优先级表和可证伪预测，用于指导基准测试的迭代方向。

<h3 id="118">[118] Graph-Driven Cross-Industry Real-Time Monitoring Framework for Anti-Money Laundering Detection in Converged Mobility-Energy Supply Chain Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18844) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rong Liu, Xiaojun Xiao, Zhanqing Su
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Fraud Detection, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个图驱动的跨行业实时反洗钱监控框架，用于融合出行-能源供应链网络。该框架构建跨行业异构图，通过时序双图注意力网络和元路径子图推理模块来识别跨行业串通洗钱行为。

<h3 id="119">[119] First-Passage Prediction of Grokking Delay: ACalibrated Law under AdamW with Causal Validation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18845) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Truong Xuan Khanh, Truong Quynh Hoa, Luu Duc Trung, Phan Thanh Duc
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Grokking, Deep Learning Theory, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文首次给出了AdamW优化器下Grokking延迟的定量预测公式，将延迟视为首次通过时间问题。该公式在多个架构和任务上得到验证，并通过因果干预确认了范数分离和角度可达性是产生延迟的必要条件。

<h3 id="120">[120] Lost and Found in Translation: Variational Diagnostics for Neural Codebook Channels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18846) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusuke Hayashi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Variational Inference, Representation Learning, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了神经码本通道诊断方法，用于验证VAE中解码器是否按照编码器的编码方式读取潜在变量。该方法提供了一个架构无关的Bernoulli-KL证书，证明了标准VAE诊断指标无法确定编码器-解码器的一致性。

<h3 id="121">[121] Transformers Linearly Represent Highly Structured World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18847](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18847) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Roman Kniazev, Nathanaël Fijalkow
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, World Model, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文通过分析数独求解任务上的Transformer，发现模型构建了围绕约束结构而非单元格的世界模型。作者识别出一个稀疏、单语义且完全可解释的"裸单电路"，展示了机制可解释性工具如何恢复Transformer解决组合推理任务的端到端算法描述。

<h3 id="122">[122] Exact Linear Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18848) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weinuo Ou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Linear Attention, Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 本文提出了精确线性注意力机制(ELA)，通过核函数的精确分解实现线性计算复杂度，无需任何近似误差。论文还提出了Hyper Link结构、Memory Lobe模块和基于路由分数的MoE偏置机制等工程创新。

<h3 id="123">[123] INSIGHTS: Demonstration-Based Summaries of Time Series Predictors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18849](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18849) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bar Eini Porat, Rom Gutman, Uri Shalit, Ofra Amir
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, Explainable AI, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了INSIGHTS，一种模型无关的、以用户为中心的时间序列模型全局解释方法。该方法通过平衡样本的重要性和多样性来创建信息丰富的子集摘要，帮助用户全面理解模型行为。

<h3 id="124">[124] KadiAssistant: A conversational AI Agent for information retrieval in Kadi4Mat</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18850](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18850) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adrian Cierpka, Mohammad Shafiqul Islam, Johannes Steinhülb, Eric Dietriche Sesso Domtchoueng, Michael Selzer, Arnd Koeppe
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Information Retrieval, Material Discovery, Privacy
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了KadiAssistant，一个集成到Kadi研究数据生态系统中的隐私优先AI助手，用于材料科学领域的信息检索。该系统结合自托管大语言模型和隐私保护语义搜索，能够处理异构、隐私敏感的研究数据。

<h3 id="125">[125] Robust Checkpoint Selection for Multimodal LLMs via Agentic Evaluation and Stability-Aware Ranking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18852) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qinwu Xu, Zhuoheng Li, Jessie Salas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Model Evaluation, OCR
- **Summary**: 本文将多模态大语言模型的检查点选择问题形式化为评估不确定性下的鲁棒决策问题，提出了一个多阶段框架。该框架整合了真实世界数据、结构化LLM判断和多阶段排序协议，并引入了基于子采样的置信度估计。

<h3 id="126">[126] The 99% Success Paradox: When Near-Perfect Retrieval Equals Random Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18857) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vyzantinos Repantis, Harshvardhan Singh, Tony Joseph, Cien Zhang, Akash Vishwakarma, Svetlana Karslioglu, Michael Wyatt Thot, Ameya Gawde
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, RAG, Model Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文引入了Bits-over-Random(BoR)指标，一种机会校正的检索选择性度量，揭示了高成功率可能掩盖随机水平性能的现象。研究发现当预期覆盖率超过3-5倍时，基线主导导致选择性崩溃，这对RAG评估和LLM代理工具选择有重要启示。

<h3 id="127">[127] When Individually Calibrated Models Become Collectively Miscalibrated</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18858](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18858) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaohui Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.GT; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Model Calibration, Multi-Agent System, Game Theory
- **Venue**: ProbML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文证明了在多智能体设置中，单独校准的预测器在预测战略性交互时会产生集体失校准现象。作者分析了Brier分数聚合下的系统性低估问题，并证明基于VCG的聚合方法可以实现占优策略激励兼容性。

<h3 id="128">[128] TwinRouterBench: Fast Static and Live Dynamic Evaluation for Realistic Agentic LLM Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18859](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18859) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pei Yang, Wanyi Chen, Tongyun Yang, Pengbin Feng, Jiarong Xing, Wentao Guo, Yuhang Yao, Yuhang Han, Hanchen Li, Xu Wang, Zeyu Wang, Jie Xiao, Anjie Yang, Liang Tian, Lynn Ai, Eric Yang, Tianyu Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, LLM Routing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CommonstackAI/TwinRouterBench)
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了TwinRouterBench，一个用于评估LLM路由器的基准测试，包含静态和动态两个赛道。静态赛道提供970个路由可见前缀，动态赛道在SWE-bench Verified上运行实时代理执行，支持快速离线迭代和端到端验证。

<h3 id="129">[129] Towards Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning on Sub-5KB Models: A Feasibility Study of Privacy-Preserving ECG Monitoring for Ultra-Resource-Constrained Wearables</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18862) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hangyu Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Medical AI, Wearable Computing
- **Summary**: 本文提出Family-FL框架，一种面向超低功耗可穿戴设备ECG监测的三层联邦学习架构，通过家庭分组聚合减少通信开销，并设计了仅669参数的量化CNN-LSTM模型，在保持准确率的同时将通信量降低至FedAvg的0.31%。

<h3 id="130">[130] SAGE: Shaping Anchors for Guided Exploration in RLVR of LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18864](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18864) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chanuk Lee, Minki Kang, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/tally0818/SAGE)
- **Summary**: 本文提出SAGE框架，通过重塑反向KL锚点分布来解决RLVR中探索不足的问题，使策略能够在保持训练稳定性的同时探索更多推理模式，在数学推理基准测试中同时提升了pass@1和pass@k性能。

<h3 id="131">[131] From Sparsity to Simplicity: Enabling Simpler Sequential Replacements via Sparse Attention Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18865) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxin Ren, Maxwell D Collins, Miao Hu, Huanrui Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Model Compression, Vision Transformer
- **Summary**: 本文基于注意力稀疏性观察，提出通过稀疏注意力蒸馏来用更简单的序列模块替换预训练ViT中的注意力层，发现替换稀疏注意力层比替换稠密层带来的精度损失更小，实现了参数量和延迟的降低。

<h3 id="132">[132] FLUIDSPLAT: Reconstructing Physical Fields from Sparse Sensors via Gaussian Primitives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18866](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18866) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huaxi Huang, Meng Li, Zhengqing Gao, Xi Zhou, Xiaoshui Huang, Xiao Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scientific Computing, Physics-Informed Learning, Digital Twin
- **Summary**: 本文提出FLUIDSPLAT方法，受3D高斯泼溅启发，利用K个各向异性高斯基元从稀疏表面传感器重建流场，提供了空间可解释的中间表示，在圆柱流和AirfRANS基准测试中取得了最佳误差表现。

<h3 id="133">[133] EVA-0: Test-Time Model Evolution with Only Two Forward Passes per Sample</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18867) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 本文提出EVA-0框架，在严格的两次前向传播预算下实现测试时模型演化，通过零阶优化方法解决快捷解、权重漂移和更新方向估计等关键障碍，无需反向传播即可完成推理和适应。

<h3 id="134">[134] DarkLLM: Learning Language-Driven Adversarial Attacks with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18868](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18868) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Multimodal Learning, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文提出DarkLLM攻击框架，训练LLM将自然语言攻击指令转换为潜在攻击向量，实现对CLIP、SAM和前沿LLM等多种基础模型的灵活可控对抗攻击生成，揭示了现代基础模型的系统性漏洞。

<h3 id="135">[135] MO-CAPO: Multi-Objective Cost-Aware Prompt Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18869) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE

- **Summary**: 本文提出MO-CAPO算法，联合优化LLM提示的性能和推理成本，通过预算分配实现成本高效的多目标优化，发现多样化的性能-成本权衡解集，使实践者能够根据需求选择合适的提示。

<h3 id="136">[136] Distributional Energy-Based Models for Uncertainty-Aware Structured LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18871](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18871) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shireen Kudukkil Manchingal, Abhey Kalia, Fernanda Gonçalves, Shebin Rawther
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出分解能量函数方法，结合学习质量评分器和确定性约束惩罚来验证LLM结构化输出，通过集成方差量化认知不确定性驱动两阶段推理循环，在多个推理基准测试中超越了大型模型。

<h3 id="137">[137] EUPHORIA: Efficient Universal Planning via Hybrid Optimization for Robust Industrial Robotic Assembly</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18872) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="138">[138] GenAI-FDIA: Physics-Informed Generative Models for False Data Injection Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18873) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="139">[139] ZeroUnlearn: Few-Shot Knowledge Unlearning in Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="140">[140] To Call or Not to Call: Diagnosing Intrinsic Over-Calling Bias in LLM Agents</h3>

<h3 id="141">[141] Prediction Is Not Physics: Learning and Evaluating Conserved Quantities in Neural Simulators</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="142">[142] The Extremum Stack is a Minimal Sufficient Statistic for Rate-Independent Functionals: A Kolmogorov Complexity Characterisation</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; cs.CC

- **Summary**: 本文证明了离散序列的极值栈是所有可计算、因果、率无关泛函类的最小充分统计量，建立了Kolmogorov复杂度意义下的最优性保证，为磁滞驱动流的压缩提供了理论依据。

<h3 id="143">[143] Soft Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18889) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Aledhari, Ali Aledhari, Fatimah Aledhari, Mohamed Rahouti

<h3 id="144">[144] Stop Drawing Scientific Claims from LLM Social Simulations Without Robustness Audits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18890) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Social Simulation, LLM Evaluation, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文指出LLM社会模拟的科学声明应基于稳健性审计，通过案例研究展示微小扰动可导致宏观结果的巨大变化，并提出TRAILS稳健性审计分类法，呼吁将稳健性作为一级验证要求。

<h3 id="145">[145] Auditing Reasoning-Trace Memorization Claims after Unlearning with Head-Conditioned Canaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18891) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanhang Li, Zhichao Fan, Zexin Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Memorization, Machine Unlearning
- **Summary**: 本文审计了推理模型在遗忘学习后的推理轨迹记忆现象，发现正向的解析器分裂旁路差距本身并不能确定是否存在隐藏的权重级记忆，也不能排除这种可能性。研究建议在标准审计之外使用解码时模板交换作为廉价的健全性检查。

<h3 id="146">[146] Data-Free Client Contribution Estimation via Logit Maximization for Federated Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18892) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Asim Ukaye, Nurbek Tastan, Mubarak Abdu-Aguye, Karthik Nandakumar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于逻辑最大化的无数据客户端贡献估计框架(CELM)，用于解决联邦学习中的类别不平衡和标签偏斜问题。该方法通过探测客户端更新获取类别证据分数，构建跨客户端证据矩阵来量化每类能力和类别覆盖度，从而改善聚合效果。

<h3 id="147">[147] KG-ASG: Collision-Knowledge-Guided Closed-Loop Adversarial Scenario Generation With Primary-Support Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18895](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18895) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cheng Wang, Chen Xiong, Ziwen Wang, Yuchen Zhou, Qiang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Knowledge Graph, Scenario Generation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了KG-ASG框架，通过构建碰撞知识库和训练轻量级碰撞专家来生成对抗性场景，用于自动驾驶系统的安全验证。该方法能够推断目标碰撞模式、主要对抗者和支持车辆及其交互角色，提高了对抗场景的有效性、可解释性和可执行性。

<h3 id="148">[148] Cross-Subject Intracranial EEG Reconstruction from Scalp Recordings Using Multi-Scale Cross-Attention Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18897) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tien-Dat Pham, Xuan-The Tran
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, EEG Processing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CAST框架，通过跨注意力时空Transformer实现跨受试者的颅内脑电重建，仅使用头皮脑电信号预测未见患者的颅内信号。该方法采用两阶段迁移学习策略，在感觉运动区域达到高达r=0.864的峰值相关性，仅需几分钟的目标受试者数据校准即可适应新的硬件配置。

<h3 id="149">[149] Don't Let Bandit Feedback Pull Continual LLM-Recommender Updates Off Target</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18899) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Taesan Kim, Hyeongjun Yun, Jaegul Choo, Chung Park
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Multi-Armed Bandit
- **Summary**: 本文提出了锚定强盗策略优化框架(ABPO)，用于生成式LLM推荐器的持续更新，解决部署日志中仅提供策略塑造的上下文强盗反馈问题。该方法结合组相对策略优化与暴露偏差处理，通过对正响应和无响应的不对称处理，有效缓解了先前策略引起的暴露偏差。

<h3 id="150">[150] Dynamic Model Merging Made Slim</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18904](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18904) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guodong Du, Wanyu Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Model Merging
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DiDi-Merging框架，通过可微分秩分配来平衡共享参数和专家参数，实现轻量化的动态模型合并。该方法仅需单微调模型1.24倍的参数即可匹配先前动态基线，在1.4倍时超越它们，适用于视觉、语言和多模态任务。

<h3 id="151">[151] Stability and Discretization Error of State Space Model Neural Operators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18905](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18905) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abderrahim Bendahi, Adrien Fradin, Johan Peralez, Julie Digne, Madiha Nadri
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE; math.NA
- **Tags**: Neural Operator, State Space Model
- **Summary**: 本文建立了神经算子近似方案的离散化误差和稳定性理论保证，证明了将解正则性与输入离散化联系起来的解析界限。研究专门针对状态空间模型神经算子(SS-NOs)和傅里叶神经算子推导了离散化误差定理，并通过输入到状态稳定性分析评估了离散化对SS-NOs稳定性的影响。

<h3 id="152">[152] Lightweight and Fast Backdoor Model Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18907) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yinbo Yu, Jing Fang, Xuewen Zhang, Chunwei Tian, Qi Zhu, Daoqiang Zhang, Jiajia Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Backdoor Detection
- **Summary**: 本文提出了DFBScanner，一种轻量级静态参数检查框架，用于快速后门扫描。该方法利用后门引起的特征扰动会导致最终分类层参数更新异常的关键观察，通过构建和组合最终层参数的多个异常指标来检测后门，在超过5000个后门模型上实现了97.17%的真阳性率和仅1毫秒的平均检测时间。

<h3 id="153">[153] Fast and Lightweight Backdoor Detection via Head Random Probing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18908) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yinbo Yu, Xueyu Yin, Jing Fang, Chunwei Tian, Qi Zhu, Jiajia Liu, Daoqiang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Backdoor Detection, Model Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 本文提出了HTell，一种快速轻量级的无数据后门检测器，通过头部随机探测技术分析类别响应统计来检测DNN中的后门，无需干净数据、梯度或参数优化，在6000多个后门模型上实现了99.03%的真阳性率和仅12.69ms的检测延迟。

<h3 id="154">[154] Does Your Wildfire Prediction Model Actually Work, or Just Score Well?</h3>

<h3 id="155">[155] SCAFDS: Edge-Feature Graph Attention for Interbank Fraud Detection with Attribution-Grounded SAR Generation</h3>

<h3 id="158">[158] ESLD (External Surrogate Latent Defense): A Latent-Space Architecture for Faster, Stronger Prompt-Injection Defense</h3>

<h3 id="161">[161] OEP: Poisoning Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Locally Correct but Non-Transferable Experiences</h3>

- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Question Answering, Multimodal Learning

- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bhosalems/CRAFT)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了CRAFT，一个用于多模态视频问答的查询条件管道，结合动态关键帧选择、ASR多语言回退和混合批评循环来迭代验证和修复声明。该方法在MAGMaR 2026数据集上取得了最佳整体平均分和引用F1，并能有效泛化到WikiVideo数据集。

<h3 id="178">[178] ReacTOD: Bounded Neuro-Symbolic Agentic NLU for Zero-Shot Dialogue State Tracking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19077) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanjun Lin, Zimo Xiao, Kartik Natarajan, Mahesh Sankaranarayanan, Niraj Nawanit, Rakshit Parashar, Austin Zhang, Karthik Konaraddi, Rishita Mote, Wei Niu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Neurosymbolic AI, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ReacTOD，一种有界神经符号架构，将自然语言理解重新表述为自纠正ReAct循环中的离散工具调用，并配合确定性验证器。该方法在MultiWOZ 2.1上实现了零样本对话状态跟踪的新SOTA，gpt-oss-20B达到52.71%联合目标准确率。

<h3 id="179">[179] MANGO: Meta-Adaptive Network Gradient Optimization for Online Continual Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ankita Awasthi, Marco Apolinario, Kaushik Roy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种反事实似然测试方法，用于测量推理系统中私有推理通道之间的间接影响。该方法通过替换上游私有块并测量下游目标的负对数似然偏移，证明了私有通道评估应分别报告直接影响和间接影响。

<h3 id="180">[180] Counterfactual Likelihood Tests for Indirect Influence in Private Reasoning Channels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19092) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander Boesgaard Lorup
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Neural Combinatorial Optimization, Diffusion Model, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 本文提出了GOAL，一种基于条件扩散求解器的动态多目标优化方法，通过异构图编码处理不同约束类型。该方法在FSP、JSP和FJSP三个调度基准上实现100%解可行性和接近零的MAPE，在解质量和推理速度上比NSGA-II和MOEA/D提升高达25倍。

<h3 id="181">[181] ScheduleFree+: Scaling Learning-Rate-Free & Schedule-Free Learning to Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19095](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19095) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aaron Defazio
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Optimization
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ScheduleFree+，一种无需学习率和调度的大语言模型训练方法，在大规模训练中显著优于传统的Warmup-Stable-Decay调度。该方法在每参数1000个token的长时训练中比SOTA调度提升31%，为预训练中的模型平均和检查点合并提供了理论基础。

<h3 id="182">[182] Neural Operators for Design-Space Surrogate Modeling of Tendon-Actuated Continuum Robots</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19104](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19104) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Branden Frieden, James M. Ferguson, Alan Kuntz, Varun Shankar
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Meta-Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了MANGO，一个在线持续学习框架，通过梯度门控和元学习正则化来平衡稳定性与可塑性。该方法在CLEAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet基准上取得了SOTA结果，并在CLEAR-10上实现了正向后向迁移。

<h3 id="183">[183] FAGER: Factually Grounded Evaluation and Refinement of Text-to-Image Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19111) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngsun Lim, Cusuh Ham, Pin-Yu Chen, Deepti Ghadiyaram
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Operator, Robotics, Surrogate Model
- **Venue**: ICRA 2026
- **Summary**: 本文将肌腱驱动连续机器人的代理建模形式化为算子学习问题，开发了四种基于DeepONet和傅里叶神经算子的架构。训练后的模型能够在不同机器人设计间快速准确地泛化，为手术和工业应用中的控制、规划和设计优化提供支持。

<h3 id="184">[184] GOAL: Graph-based Objective-Aligned Diffusion Solvers for Dynamic Multi-Objective Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Li
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, LLM Evaluation, Image Generation
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 本文提出了FAGER，一个用于评估和改进文本到图像模型事实正确性的代理框架，通过构建结构化事实评分表并进行VLM评估。该方法在科学、历史、产品和文化等五个数据集上的一致性A/B测试中优于先前指标，并可用于无训练的图像生成改进。

<h3 id="185">[185] EgoBabyVLM: Benchmarking Cross-Modal Learning from Naturalistic Egocentric Video Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19130](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19130) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongyan Lin, Phillip Rust, Angel Villar Corrales, Alvin W. M. Tan, Mahi Luthra, Charles-Éric Saint-James, Rashel Moritz, Sheila Krogh-Jespersen, Vanessa Stark, Surya Parimi, Jiayi Shen, Youssef Benchekroun, Yosuke Higuchi, Martin Gleize, Tom Fizycki, Nicolas Hamilakis, Manel Khentout, Sho Tsuji, Balázs Kégl, Juan Pino, Michael C. Frank, Emmanuel Dupoux
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Benchmark, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 本文引入EgoBabyVLM基准，用于评估视觉语言模型从自然主义自我中心视频数据进行跨模态学习的能力。研究发现当前VLM范式依赖于精心策划数据的紧密语义对齐，无法利用自然主义输入中的弱对齐信号，并提出了Machine-DevBench来测量词汇和语法能力。

<h3 id="186">[186] Knowing When Not to Predict: Self Supervised Learning and Abstention for Safer DR Screening</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19133) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muskaan Chopra, Lorenz Sparrenberg, Jan H. Terheyden, Rafet Sifa
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Self-Supervised Learning, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究了自监督学习预训练时长对糖尿病视网膜病变筛查中校准置信度和弃权行为的影响。研究发现SSL预训练相比从头训练提高了选择性预测性能，但准确率饱和后选择性性能仍可能显著变化，更长的预训练并不一致地提高可靠性。

<h3 id="187">[187] COBALT: Crowdsourcing Robot Learning via Cloud-Based Teleoperation with Smartphones</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19138](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19138) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ayush Agarwal, Ansh Gandhi, Jeremy A. Collins, Omar Rayyan, Aryan Sarswat, Ranjani Koushik, Masoud Moghani, Ajay Mandlekar, Animesh Garg
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning, Robotics, Crowdsourcing
- **Summary**: 本文提出了COBALT，一个基于云的遥操作平台，支持使用智能手机等常见设备进行大规模众包机器人学习。系统支持多用户并发遥操作，在单GPU上以20Hz频率支持多达8个并发用户，并通过用户研究表明手机遥操作性能可与专用硬件媲美。

<h3 id="188">[188] GRASP: Deterministic argument ranking in interaction graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19141) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Diganta Misra, Antonio Orvieto, Rediet Abebe, Volkan Cevher
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Computational Argumentation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了GRASP，一个确定性论证排序框架，通过攻击-防御传播算子将稳定的局部交互判断聚合为全局排序。研究表明局部交互判断比整体排序更具可复现性，GRASP分数衡量的是结构充分性而非说服力或事实性，为整体LLM评判提供了透明可审计的替代方案。

<h3 id="189">[189] Be Kind, Rewrite: Benign Projections via Rewriting Defend Against LLM Data Poisoning Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19147) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: John T. Halloran, Noopur S. Bhatt
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Data Poisoning, Backdoor Attack
- **Summary**: 本文提出了开放书本良性重写(OBBR)作为防御LLM数据投毒攻击的方法，利用LLM重写配合良性样本将训练样本投影到良性提示空间。该方法在五种已知后门攻击和四种LLM上，平均比SOTA后门防御提高51%的安全性性能。

<h3 id="190">[190] Flash PD-SSM: Memory-Optimized Structured Sparse State-Space Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19150](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19150) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: State Space Model, Efficient Architecture, Time Series Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Flash PD-SSM，一种结构化稀疏状态空间模型，在保持与结构化SSM相当吞吐量的同时实现更好的表达能力保证。该模型在每个时间步离散选择结构化稀疏矩阵，在超过17000长度的多变量时间序列任务上取得SOTA准确率，并可作为混合LLM的有效替代。

<h3 id="191">[191] Bridge: Retrieval-Augmented Spatiotemporal Modeling for Urban Delivery Demand</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19172](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19172) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yihong Tang, Tong Nie, Junlin He, Qianjun Huang, Dingyi Zhuang, Lijun Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, RAG, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Bridge，一个检索增强的时空图框架，用于城市配送需求的冷启动区域预测。该方法结合归纳上下文图骨干和时间感知记忆检索，通过门控融合机制优化预测，在城市内冷启动和跨城市迁移场景中持续优于竞争基线。

<h3 id="192">[192] Planner-Admissible Graph-PDE Value Extensions for Sparse Goal-Conditioned Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19185](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19185) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shiheng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Planning, Graph Learning, Value Learning
- **Summary**: 本文研究了稀疏目标条件规划中的图-PDE值扩展问题，提出了局部动作间隙证书来判断规划器可接受性。绝对最小Lipschitz扩展(AMLE)在AntMaze配置上达到0.970的滚动成功率，显著优于调和扩展的0.584。

<h3 id="193">[193] Going PLACES: Participatory Localized Red Teaming for Text-to-Image Safety in the Global South</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19190) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Charvi Rastogi, Mukul Bhutani, Minsuk Kahng, Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad, Evgeniia Razumovskaia, Priyanka Suresh, Ibrahim Said Ahmad, Charu Kalia, Yaaseen Mahomed, Madhurima Maji, Minjae Lee, Alicia Parrish, Jessica Quaye, Vijay Janapa Reddi, Aishwarya Verma, Lora Aroyo
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Red Teaming, AI Safety
- **Venue**: FAccT 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PLACES数据集，包含超过26,000个文本到图像模型失败案例，通过与全球南方（加纳、尼日利亚、印度）的大学合作进行本地化社区红队测试收集。研究发现现有安全框架存在结构性缺陷，无法识别由当地文化和语言细微差别引发的独特对抗模式和新型危害。

<h3 id="194">[194] On-Device Continual Learning with Dual-Stage Buffer and Dynamic Loss for Point-of-Care Pneumonia Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19201](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19201) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Danu Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Medical AI, On-Device Learning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PneumoNet，一种用于即时护理肺炎诊断的域增量学习方法，结合轻量级CNN、双阶段平衡缓冲区和动态类加权损失。该方法在域偏移场景下实现了86.6%的准确率和1.4%的遗忘率，适合在资源受限的医疗设备上部署。

<h3 id="195">[195] Aerial Inspection Behaviors via RL-based Quadrotor Control for Under-canopy Forest Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19202) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fausto Mauricio Lagos Suarez, Akshit Saradagi, Vidya Sumathy, Viswa Narayanan Sankaranarayanan, George Nikolakopoulos
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Robotics, Path Planning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的四旋翼无人机端到端控制器，用于林冠下森林环境的空中巡检任务。该系统结合了TSP规划器和RRT*规划器的高层导航引导层，实现了巡检视角姿态跟踪和长距离安全飞行。

<h3 id="196">[196] Quantized Machine Learning Models for Medical Imaging in Low-Resource Healthcare Settings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19207) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sumanth Meenan Kanneti, Aryan Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种用于脑肿瘤MRI分类的多策略压缩框架，包括量化感知训练、知识蒸馏和Float16后训练量化。量化后的MobileNetV2模型实现了82.37%的验证准确率，模型大小压缩6.14倍，适合资源受限的医疗环境部署。

<h3 id="197">[197] Rotation-Aligned Key Channel Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Model Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19218) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了RotateK，一种基于旋转的结构化Key通道剪枝框架，用于视觉语言模型的高效推理。该方法通过在线PCA旋转将通道重要性对齐到共享低维子空间，在保持准确性的同时显著降低解码延迟。

<h3 id="198">[198] Position: Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs is Just Unsupervised Clustering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19220) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tiejin Chen, Longchao Da, Xiaoou Liu, Hua Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Hallucination
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文指出大语言模型的主流不确定性量化方法本质上是无监督聚类算法，仅量化模型生成的内部一致性而非外部正确性。作者识别了三个关键病理问题，并呼吁研究社区采用更好的评估指标和范式转变。

<h3 id="199">[199] Token by Token, Compromised: Backdoor Vulnerabilities in Unified Autoregressive Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19227) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tobias Braun, Jonas Henry Grebe, Hossein Shakibania, Anna Rohrbach, Marcus Rohrbach
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, Vision-Language Model, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 本文首次展示了统一自回归模型的多模态后门漏洞，提出了ToBAC攻击方法，可通过无害字符或常见词触发跨模态恶意行为。攻击在无模型访问时达到63.1%的成功率，揭示了统一架构的新型安全风险。

<h3 id="200">[200] Diagnosing Multi-step Reasoning Failures in Black-box LLMs via Stepwise Confidence Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19228](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19228) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoou Liu, Tiejin Chen, Dengjia Zhang, Yaqing Wang, Lu Cheng, Hua Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IT; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Evaluation, Self-Correction
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出了逐步置信度归因(SCA)框架，用于诊断黑盒LLM的多步推理失败。该方法基于信息瓶颈原理为推理步骤分配置信度，有效识别低置信度步骤，并将自纠正成功率提升高达13.5%。

<h3 id="201">[201] AI Technologies in Language Access: Attitudes Towards AI and the Human Value of Language Access Managers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19234) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Miguel A. Jiménez-Crespo, Stephanie Rodriguez, Alejandro Jaume Losa
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, AI Ethics, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 本文研究了语言服务管理者对AI技术的态度，通过定性访谈分析发现他们表现出有条件的乐观态度、强烈的风险意识，以及对AI实施中人类价值和监督的深度承诺。

<h3 id="202">[202] PhyWorld: Physics-Faithful World Model for Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19242) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pu Zhao, Juyi Lin, Timothy Rupprecht, Arash Akbari, Chence Yang, Rahul Chowdhury, Elaheh Motamedi, Arman Akbari, Yumei He, Chen Wang, Geng Yuan, Weiwei Chen, Yanzhi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: World Model, Video Generation, Physical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出了PhyWorld，一种通过两阶段后训练生成物理忠实视频的世界模型。第一阶段使用流匹配微调改善视频连续性，第二阶段通过物理偏好对齐生成动态，在视频一致性和物理合理性方面均取得提升。

<h3 id="203">[203] Euclidean Embedding of Data Using Local Distances</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19243](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19243) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dimitris Arabadjis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CG
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Dimensionality Reduction, Graph Learning
- **Summary**: 本文研究仅给定局部距离图恢复数据欧几里得嵌入的问题，提出了一种通过变分问题匹配局部距离与欧几里得度量的方法。该方法无需先验向量表示，仅依赖邻域图操作即可实现全局等距嵌入的近似。

<h3 id="204">[204] ExECG: An Explainable AI Framework for ECG models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jong-Hwan Jang, Yong-yeon Jo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Medical AI, ECG Analysis
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ExECG，一个用于ECG模型的可解释AI框架，提供三阶段流水线：Wrapper标准化访问、Explainer统一多种XAI方法、Visualizer支持跨方法比较。该框架促进了ECG可解释性的互操作性和可复现性。

<h3 id="205">[205] When Web Apps Heal Themselves: A MAPE-K Based Approach to Fault Tolerance and Adaptive Recovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19261) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sales Aribe Jr, Rov Japheth Oracion
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Fault Tolerance, Self-Healing System, Autonomous Systems
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种基于MAPE-K模型的自愈框架，用于Web应用的容错和自适应恢复。系统在20种运行时故障场景中实现了90.7%的故障检测F1分数和93.2%的恢复成功率，平均恢复时间减少56.2%。

<h3 id="206">[206] FormalASR: End-to-End Spoken Chinese to Formal Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19266](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19266) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wanyi Ning, Yinshang Guo, Haitao Qian, Jiyuan Cheng, Weiyuan Feng, Yufei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 本文提出了FormalASR，一种端到端模型，可直接将口语中文转录为正式书面文本，无需两阶段ASR+LLM流水线。模型在保持轻量化的同时实现了高达37.4%的相对CER降低，适合设备端部署。

<h3 id="207">[207] EviTrack: Selection over Sampling for Delayed Disambiguation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19283) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Omer Haq
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Sequential Inference, State Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Haq94/EviTrack)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了EviTrack，一种用于延迟消歧场景的测试时推理框架，通过维护竞争轨迹假设并应用基于证据的选择来延迟决策。在相同推理预算下，该方法显著优于采样基线，实现更快的消歧后恢复。

<h3 id="208">[208] Are Rationales Necessary and Sufficient? Tuning LLMs for Explainable Misinformation Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19285](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19285) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 本文提出了LONSREX数据合成流水线，用于微调可解释虚假信息检测的LLM。该方法量化每个验证步骤对最终预测的贡献，识别必要且充分的理由，解决了朴素过滤产生不足或不必要理由的问题。

<h3 id="209">[209] DEFLECT: Delay-Robust Execution via Flow-matching Likelihood-Estimated Counterfactual Tuning for VLA Policies</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文针对视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略在异步推理中产生的预测-执行错位问题，提出了DEFLECT方法。该方法通过反事实动作对和流匹配似然估计进行离线后训练优化，在高延迟场景下显著提升了控制成功率。

<h3 id="210">[210] ContextFlow: Hierarchical Task-State Alignment for Long-Horizon Embodied Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19314](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19314) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Task Planning, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 本文针对长时程具身智能体中的任务状态错位问题，提出了ContextFlow框架，通过将阶段表示为显式契约、将运行时观察转换为证据包，并应用作用域更新来维护规划、监控、记忆和执行之间的任务前沿一致性。

<h3 id="211">[211] Inference-Time Scaling in Diffusion Models through Iterative Partial Refinement</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Test-Time Adaptation, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ahn-ml/IPR)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种无需外部验证器的推理时扩展方法——迭代部分细化(IPR)，用于序列扩散模型。该方法通过重新加噪和再生部分区域来优化全局一致性，在MNIST数独任务上将有效解率从55.8%提升至75.0%。

<h3 id="212">[212] Exploring and Developing a Pre-Model Safeguard with Draft Models</h3>

<h3 id="213">[213] RE-VLM: Event-Augmented Vision-Language Model for Scene Understanding</h3>

<h3 id="214">[214] STAR-PólyaMath: Multi-Agent Reasoning under Persistent Meta-Strategic Supervision</h3>

<h3 id="215">[215] HalluWorld: A Controlled Benchmark for Hallucination via Reference World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19341](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19341) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="216">[216] IMLJD: A Computational Dataset for Indian Matrimonial Litigation Analysis</h3>

<h3 id="217">[217] PAVE: A Cognitive Architecture for Legitimate Violation in Generative Agent Societies</h3>

<h3 id="218">[218] Brain alignment of reasoning and action representations from vision-language and action models during naturalistic gameplay</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19352) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="219">[219] Skinned Motion Retargeting with Spatially Adaptive Interaction Guidance</h3>

<h3 id="220">[220] Toward User Comprehension Supports for LLM Agent Skill Specifications</h3>

<h3 id="221">[221] Multi-Scale Generative Modeling with Heat Dissipation Flow Matching</h3>

<h3 id="222">[222] Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types for Neural Network Model Merging: A Two-Layer Architecture Enabling CRDT-Compliant Model Merging Across 26 Strategies</h3>

<h3 id="223">[223] Concept-Guided Noisy Negative Suppression for Zero-Shot Classification and Grounding of Chest X-Ray Findings</h3>

<h3 id="224">[224] The Evaluation Game: Beyond Static LLM Benchmarking</h3>

<h3 id="225">[225] EmbGen: Teaching with Reassembled Corpora</h3>

<h3 id="226">[226] Rebalancing Reference Frame Dominance to Improve Motion in Image-to-Video Models</h3>

<h3 id="227">[227] A Bitter Lesson for Data Filtering</h3>

<h3 id="228">[228] Unlocking the Potential of Continual Model Merging: An ODE Perspective</h3>

<h3 id="229">[229] When to Stop Reusing: Dynamic Gradient Gating for Sample-Efficient RLVR</h3>

<h3 id="230">[230] Backtracking When It Strays: Mitigating Dual Exposure Biases in LLM Reasoning Distillation</h3>

<h3 id="231">[231] KappaPlace: Learning Hyperspherical Uncertainty for Visual Place Recognition via Prototype-Anchored Supervision</h3>

<h3 id="232">[232] When the Majority Votes Wrong, the Intervention Timing for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning Hides in the Extinction Window</h3>

<h3 id="234">[234] Resilient Byzantine Agreement with Predictions</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了CANINE系统，用于训练视障用户与机器导盲犬进行交互式导航。该系统通过知识追踪跟踪学习者的子技能熟练度，并利用基础模型推断错误原因、生成针对性的自适应语言反馈，显著提升了学习效率和导航性能。

<h3 id="239">[239] ARC-RL: A Reinforcement Learning Playground Inspired by ARC Raiders</h3>

<h3 id="240">[240] Base Models Look Human To AI Detectors</h3>

<h3 id="241">[241] Investigating Cross-Modal Skill Injection: Scenarios, Methods, and Hyperparameters</h3>

<h3 id="242">[242] CaptchaMind: Training CAPTCHA Solvers via Reinforcement Learning with Explicit Reasoning Supervision</h3>

<h3 id="243">[243] EgoCoT-Bench: Benchmarking Grounded and Verifiable Operation-Centric Chain of Thought Reasoning for MLLMs</h3>

<h3 id="244">[244] TORQ: Two-Level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization</h3>

<h3 id="245">[245] Lens Privacy Sealing: A New Benchmark and Method for Physical Privacy-Preserving Action Recognition</h3>

<h3 id="246">[246] Implicit Action Chunking for Smooth Continuous Control</h3>

<h3 id="248">[248] Spectral Integrated Gradients for Coarse-to-Fine Feature Attribution</h3>

<h3 id="249">[249] MiMuon: Mixed Muon Optimizer with Improved Generalization for Large Models</h3>

<h3 id="253">[253] The Accessibility Capability Boundary: Operational Limits and Expansion Potential of AI-Generated Browser-Native Accessibility Systems</h3>

<h3 id="254">[254] CriterAlign: Criterion-Centric Rationale Alignment for Code Preference Judging</h3>

<h3 id="255">[255] Measuring Safety Alignment Effects in Autonomous Security Agents</h3>

<h3 id="256">[256] Mathematical Reasoning in Large Language Models: Benchmarks, Architectures, Evaluation, and Open Challenges</h3>

<h3 id="257">[257] LIFT and PLACE: A Simple, Stable, and Effective Knowledge Distillation Framework for Lightweight Diffusion Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种用于扩散模型知识蒸馏的粗到细框架LIFT和PLACE，通过线性拟合蒸馏和分段局部自适应系数估计来解决教师网络去噪过程过于复杂导致学生模型难以模仿的问题。该方法在极端压缩条件下（学生模型仅为教师模型的1.6%）仍能保持稳定收敛，FID达到15.73。

<h3 id="258">[258] ContextRAG: Extraction-Free Hierarchical Graph Construction for Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>

<h3 id="259">[259] TERGAD: Structure-Aware Text-Enhanced Representations for Graph Anomaly Detection</h3>

<h3 id="260">[260] Operationalising Artificial Intelligence Bills of Materials (AIBOMs) for Verifiable AI Provenance and Lifecycle Assurance</h3>

<h3 id="263">[263] AffectAI-Capture: A Reproducible Multimodal Protocol for Small-Group Meeting Research</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.AP; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Time Series Forecasting, Domain Generalization
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了FLUXtrapolation基准，用于评估生态系统通量在分布偏移下的外推能力，定义了时间、空间和温度三种外推场景。该基准揭示了现有方法在标准指标下表现相似，但在尾部误差和多尺度评估下存在差异，为分布偏移下的机器学习研究提供了现实挑战。

<h3 id="269">[269] LP-Eval: Rubric and Dataset for Measuring the Quality of Legal Proposition Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19815](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19815) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shanshan Xu, Johan Lindholm, Amogh Raina, Henrik Palmer Olsen, Daniel Hershcovich
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了LP-Eval评估标准和数据集，用于衡量欧盟法院判决法律命题生成的质量，与法律专家共同设计了包含形式有效性和实质维度的三步评估标准。研究发现LLM能生成高质量命题，但基于标准的LLM评估对细粒度差异的敏感性仍不及人类专家。

<h3 id="270">[270] ST-TGExplainer: Disentangling Stability and Transition Patterns for Temporal GNN Interpretability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19822) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongjiang Chen, Xin Zheng, Pengfei Jiao, Huan Liu, Zhidong Zhao, Huaming Wu, Feng Xia, Shirui Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Interpretability, Explainable AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hjchen-hdu/ST-TGExplainer)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了ST-TGExplainer，一种可解释的时序图神经网络，通过解耦稳定性模式（历史交互）和转移模式（新出现的交互）提供更忠实的解释。该方法使用解耦信息瓶颈目标学习紧凑的解释子图，同时抑制两种模式之间的标签条件冗余。

<h3 id="271">[271] Smooth Piecewise Cutting for Neural Operator to Handle Discontinuities and Sharp Transitions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19823](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19823) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ha Dang, Sebastian Schmidt, Juergen Hesser
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.AP; math.DS; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Neural Operator, Scientific Computing, PDE Solver
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Cut-DeepONet两阶段训练框架，通过提升策略将域划分为平滑子区域并将不连续性表示为高维空间边界，显式建模神经算子中的不连续性。该方法与神经网络归纳偏置对齐，在低分辨率数据上训练即可超越最先进方法，同时使用更少的可训练参数。

<h3 id="272">[272] Mega-ASR: Towards In-the-wild^2 Speech Recognition via Scaling up Real-world Acoustic Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19833) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhifei Xie, Kaiyu Pang, Haobin Zhang, Deheng Ye, Xiaobin Hu, Shuicheng Yan, Chunyan Miao
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MM; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Recognition, Data Augmentation, Robustness Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出了Mega-ASR框架，结合可扩展复合数据构建和渐进式声学到语义优化，实现真实环境下的鲁棒语音识别。该方法引入覆盖54种复合声学场景的200万样本数据集，在复杂声学场景上相比基线实现超过30%的相对词错误率降低。

<h3 id="273">[273] A Closed-loop, State-centric, Multi-agent Framework for Passenger Load Estimation from Heterogeneous Data Streams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19834) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiyao Xu, Hao Zhou, Yuhang Wang, Jingran Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Sensor Fusion, Autonomous Driving
- **Venue**: ITSC 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种闭环、以状态为中心的多智能体框架，用于从异构数据流中估计公交乘客负载轨迹。该方法在每个步骤强制物理可行性，动态分配证据源之间的信任，并将物理违规残差反馈到训练中以提升鲁棒性。

<h3 id="274">[274] CADENet: Condition-Adaptive Asynchronous Dual-Stream Enhancement Network for Adverse Weather Perception in Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19837) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sherif Khairy, Catherine M. Elias
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Object Detection, Computer Vision
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了CADENet，一种免训练的三线程系统，用于自动驾驶中恶劣天气下的目标检测。系统通过条件自适应增强和熵引导NMS融合，在不增加延迟的情况下提升检测性能。

<h3 id="275">[275] FineBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Vision-Language Models for Fine-grained Human Activity Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19846](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19846) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Video Understanding, Benchmark, Human Activity Recognition
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Workshop
- **Summary**: 该论文介绍了FineBench，一个专门用于评估细粒度人类活动理解的视频问答基准，包含199,420个多选题QA对。论文还提出了FineAgent框架，通过定位器和描述符增强VLM的细粒度理解能力。

<h3 id="276">[276] A Framework for Evaluating Zero-Shot Image Generation in Concept-based Explainability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19855](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19855) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Text-to-Image, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DataSciencePolimi/ZeroShot-T2I-Concepts)
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了使用零样本文本到图像生成模型作为概念数据集来源，用于基于概念的可解释AI方法。通过四种互补分析评估合成概念与真实概念的忠实度，揭示了当前T2I模型在概念XAI应用中的挑战。

<h3 id="277">[277] StableGrad: Backward Scale Control without Batch Normalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19856) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Optimization, Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了StableGrad，一种优化器级别的尺度控制机制，用于在不需要批归一化的情况下训练深度神经网络。该方法特别适用于物理信息神经网络(PINNs)，在反向传播后、优化器更新前进行归一化，保持网络输出和物理残差不变。

<h3 id="278">[278] Passive Construction Site Safety Monitoring via Persona-Scaffolded Adversarial Chain-of-Thought VLM Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ananth Sriram, Neel Mokaria, Rajveer Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Chain-of-Thought, Construction AI, Safety Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ananthsriram1/ironsite-hackathon-project-safety_assistant)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种被动的建筑工地安全监控流程，通过三阶段架构处理视频：YOLO检测、SAM分割和VLM验证。主要贡献是设计了角色脚手架对抗思维链协议，在违规检测中实现了12%的精度提升。

<h3 id="279">[279] Deep Tech to Space: Space Data Centers and AI Revolution at the Edge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19892) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonas Weiss, Patricia Sagmeister, Gabriel Maiolini Capez, Dinesh Verma, Roberto Garello, Alberto Perotti, Dawid Lazaj, Alicja Musial, Jakub Nalepa, Thomas Morf, Martin Schmatz, Marek Krawczyk, Mateusz Przeliorz, Kevin Roche, Sagar Tayal, Mahalakshmi Lakshminarayanan, Nicolas Longépé, Pierre-Philippe Mathieu, Agata Wijata

<h3 id="280">[280] Fast and Featureless Node Representation Learning with Partial Pairwise Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19916) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Contrastive FUSE，一个快速统一的图节点表示学习框架，可在仅有部分成对节点标签且无节点特征的情况下工作。该方法直接优化谱对比目标，整合社区感知的结构信号和有符号成对约束。

<h3 id="281">[281] Real-Time Parallel Counterfactual Regret Minimization</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="282">[282] Breaking Modality Heterogeneity in Low-Bit Quantization for Large Vision-Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="283">[283] StruMPL: Multi-task Dense Regression under Disjoint Partial Supervision and MNAR Labels</h3>

<h3 id="284">[284] A Measure-Theoretic Analysis of Reasoning: Structural Generalization and Approximation Limits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19944) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuyang Zhang, Yifu Zhang, Xuehai Zhou, Xiaoyin Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CC; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Deep Learning Theory, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文通过最优传输形式化推理，将离散轨迹投影到连续度量空间，使用Wasserstein-1距离量化域偏移。研究揭示了位置编码和电路深度对分布外泛化的约束，并通过54种Transformer配置的实验验证了理论界限。

<h3 id="285">[285] GEM: GPU-Variability-Aware Expert to GPU Mapping for MoE Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19945](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19945) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Inference, LLM Serving
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了GEM，一种考虑GPU变异性的专家到GPU映射框架，用于混合专家(MoE)模型服务。该方法根据GPU性能变异性和令牌负载分布，将一致性和时间性专家放置在不同GPU上，平均提升端到端延迟7.9%。

<h3 id="286">[286] World-Ego Modeling for Long-Horizon Evolution in Hybrid Embodied Tasks</h3>

<h3 id="287">[287] Detecting Fluent Optimization-Based Adversarial Prompts via Sequential Entropy Changes</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 该论文将对抗性后缀检测建模为令牌级熵流的在线变点检测问题，提出了CPD检测器。该方法使用CUSUM统计量，在六个开源聊天模型上相比最强基线提升了F1分数，并能定位对抗性后缀的起始位置。

<h3 id="288">[288] Block-Sphere Vector Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.19972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19972) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="289">[289] Learning with Foresight: Enhancing Neural Routing Policy via Multi-Node Lookahead Prediction</h3>

<h3 id="290">[290] A Case for Agentic Tuning: From Documentation to Action in PostgreSQL</h3>

<h3 id="291">[291] LLM Benchmark Datasets Should Be Contamination-Resistant</h3>

<h3 id="292">[292] Training Neural Networks with Optimal Double-Bayesian Learning</h3>

<h3 id="293">[293] When Critics Disagree: Adaptive Reward Poisoning Attacks in RIS-Aided Wireless Control System</h3>

<h3 id="294">[294] Does Code Cleanliness Affect Coding Agents? A Controlled Minimal-Pair Study</h3>

<h3 id="295">[295] PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling</h3>

<h3 id="296">[296] Towards LLM-Assisted Architecture Recovery for Real-World ROS~2 Systems: An Agent-Based Multi-Level Approach to Hierarchical Structural Architecture Reconstruction</h3>

<h3 id="297">[297] CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="298">[298] Probability-Conserving Flow Guidance</h3>

<h3 id="299">[299] VL-DPO: Vision-Language-Guided Finetuning for Preference-Aligned Autonomous Driving</h3>

<h3 id="301">[301] What Do Evolutionary Coding Agents Evolve?</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文提出CRAFT，一个红队对齐框架，利用对比表示学习和强化学习在隐藏状态空间中分离安全和 unsafe 推理轨迹。该方法在推理安全性上平均提升79%，在最终响应安全性上提升87.7%。

- **Summary**: 本文提出了一个计算框架，用于建模家庭暴力受害者在社交媒体上的自我披露行为和社区支持机制。该框架包含自我披露检测、帖子聚类、主题摘要和支持提取映射四个关键组件，为受害者中心的数字干预提供支持。

<h3 id="364">[364] ORCA: An Agentic Reasoning Framework for Hallucination and Adversarial Robustness in Vision-Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="365">[365] YAC: Bridging Natural Language and Interactive Visual Exploration with Generative AI for Biomedical Data Discovery</h3>

<h3 id="367">[367] HiDe: Rethinking The Zoom-IN method in High Resolution MLLMs via Hierarchical Decoupling</h3>

<h3 id="368">[368] Hybrid Training for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 本文系统分析了扩散模型强化学习的设计空间，发现基于ELBO的似然估计是有效优化的主导因素。该方法在90 GPU小时内将GenEval分数从0.24提升至0.95，效率显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="395">[395] Protein Autoregressive Modeling via Multiscale Structure Generation</h3>

- **Tags**: Protein Engineering, Autoregressive Model, Flow Matching

<h3 id="396">[396] Learning Rate Matters: Vanilla LoRA May Suffice for LLM Fine-tuning</h3>

<h3 id="397">[397] How does longer temporal context enhance multimodal narrative video processing in the brain?</h3>

<h3 id="398">[398] Language Model Memory and Memory Models for Language</h3>

<h3 id="399">[399] A Geometric Analysis of Small-sized Language Model Hallucinations</h3>

<h3 id="400">[400] DLEBench: Evaluating Small-scale Object Editing Ability for Instruction-based Image Editing Model</h3>

<h3 id="401">[401] 3D aperture-engineered diffractive neural networks for super-resolution electromagnetic wave computing</h3>

<h3 id="402">[402] How to Model AI Agents as Personas?: Applying the Persona Ecosystem Playground to 41,300 Posts on Moltbook for Behavioral Insights</h3>

<h3 id="403">[403] ViroGym: Realistic Large-Scale Benchmarks for Evaluating Viral Proteins</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.06740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06740) (replaced)

<h3 id="404">[404] Does AI See like Art Historians? Interpreting How Vision Language Models Recognize Artistic Style</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.11024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11024) (replaced)

<h3 id="405">[405] Synthetic Data Generation for Brain-Computer Interfaces: Overview, Benchmarking, and Future Directions</h3>

<h3 id="406">[406] How do LLMs Compute Verbal Confidence</h3>

<h3 id="409">[409] Learning Stable Predictors from Weak Supervision under Distribution Shift</h3>

<h3 id="419">[419] MoBayes: A Modular Bayesian Framework for Separating Reasoning from Language in Conversational Clinical Decision Support</h3>

<h3 id="428">[428] RELO: Reinforcement Learning to Localize for Visual Object Tracking</h3>

<h3 id="429">[429] Memory-Efficient Looped Transformer: Decoupling Compute from Memory in Looped Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="431">[431] HoReN: Normalized Hopfield Retrieval for Large-Scale Sequential Model Editing</h3>

<h3 id="434">[434] Protocol-Driven Development: Governing Generated Software Through Invariants and Continuous Evidence</h3>

<h3 id="451">[451] Learning-Zone Energy: Online Data Selection for Efficient RL Post-Training</h3>

<h3 id="452">[452] 1GC-7RC: One Graphic Card -- Seven Research Challenges! How Good Are AI Agents at Doing Your Job?</h3>

<h3 id="453">[453] DiagEval: Trajectory-Conditioned Diagnosis for Reliable Software Evaluation with GUI Agents</h3>

<h3 id="454">[454] Balancing Knowledge Distillation for Imbalance Learning with Bilevel Optimization</h3>

<h3 id="455">[455] A Simplex Witness Certificate for Constant Collapse in Variational Autoencoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18224) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zegu Zhang, Jianhua Peng, Jian Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Generative Model, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了变分自编码器（VAE）中的常数坍塌问题，并提出了一种基于单纯形见证者的方法来检测和防止这种坍塌。通过构建固定的教师后验和潜在见证者，该方法能够证明编码器均值是否依赖于输入，从而为避免模型坍塌提供了理论依据和设计原则。

<h3 id="456">[456] What's Holding Back Latent Visual Reasoning?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18445) (replaced)
- **Authors**: André G. Viveiros, Nuno Gonçalves, André F. T. Martins, Matthias Lindemann
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 该论文调查了视觉语言模型中潜在视觉推理进展缓慢的原因，发现现有模型往往忽略了潜在标记，且生成的潜在表示质量较低。研究指出，当前数据集中潜在标记提供的信息有限以及推理时潜在标记的坍缩是主要瓶颈，建议开发高质量数据集和改进潜在标记预测方法。

- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Model Fingerprinting, IP Protection
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Prompt2Fingerprint (P2F)框架，通过文本到权重的生成技术实现即插即用的大语言模型指纹注入。该方法无需重新训练模型即可快速生成身份指纹参数，有效解决了现有指纹方法计算成本高和部署延迟的问题。

<h3 id="458">[458] MINTEval: Evaluating Memory under Multi-Target Interference in Long-Horizon Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18565) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hyunji Lee, Justin Chih-Yao Chen, Joykirat Singh, Zaid Khan, Elias Stengel-Eskin, Mohit Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/amy-hyunji/MINTEval)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了MINTEval基准，用于评估长期代理系统在多目标干扰下的记忆能力。该基准包含频繁更新的长上下文信息，测试了代理在状态跟踪、多轮对话等场景下的单目标回忆和多目标聚合推理能力，揭示了现有记忆系统在处理干扰和更新信息方面的不足。

<h3 id="459">[459] Stochastic Penalty-Barrier Methods for Constrained Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18618](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18618) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Adam Bosák, Andrii Kliachkin, Jana Lepšová, Gilles Bareilles, Jakub Mareček
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Constrained Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了随机惩罚-障碍法（SPBM），用于解决深度学习中非凸、非光滑和随机设置下的约束机器学习问题。该方法通过指数对偶平均和Moreau包络技术，在保持线性运行时间开销的同时，实现了优于现有基线的性能。

<h3 id="460">[460] Vision-OPD: Learning to See Fine Details for Multimodal LLMs via On-Policy Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18740) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qianhao Yuan, Jie Lou, Xing Yu, Hongyu Lin, Le Sun, Xianpei Han, Yaojie Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Distillation, Fine-Grained Recognition
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VisionOPD/Vision-OPD)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了Vision-OPD框架，通过策略自蒸馏技术提升多模态大语言模型的细粒度视觉理解能力。该方法将模型在裁剪图像上的感知能力迁移到全图策略上，无需外部教师模型或推理时工具，显著提升了模型在细节识别任务上的表现。
